#### `double runMongoBench(const std::string &json_file, bool dry_run, bool is_json_array, double *bytes_out=nullptr)`
- Source: `benchmarks/importers/bench_importer_throughput.cpp`:489
- Brief: n/a

  - `json_file` (const std::string &): n/a
  - `dry_run` (bool): n/a
  - `is_json_array` (bool): n/a
  - `bytes_out` (double *): n/a
- Details: Run a minimal MongoDB NDJSON/JSON-array parsing pass to measure I/O and line-detection throughput. This is a simplified line-counting benchmark (not a full JSON parse or BSON type unwrap) that establishes the upper bound on import throughput; actual MongoDBImporter overhead adds JSON parsing, BSON unwrapping, and schema mapping on top of this baseline. Returns wall-clock elapsed time in seconds; sets *bytes_out to total bytes read.
